Pre decla
lateDeri
Bustom
 MON mapping helper derived from the stable SAP map/dump pattern.
 Image-confirmed semantic targets:
   MON_MATERIAL_ID       Product / Material input
   MON_BIN_ID            Storage Bin input
   MON_STATUS_ID         Warehouse Task Status filter
   MON_RESULT_GRID_ID    Warehouse Task result grid
   MON_WT_COL             Whse Order column
   MON_OB_COL             Document / OB column
   MON_MATERIAL_COL       Product / Material column
   MON_BATCH_COL          Batch column
   MON_BIN_COL            Source Bin column
   MON_QTY_COL            SrcTgtQty / Dest.Qty candidate column
   MON_STATUS_COL         Status column
   MON_CANCEL_ID          Cancel WT action
   MON_CANCEL_CONFIRM_ID  Cancel confirmation button/popup
 The routine writes real SAP GUI IDs discovered from the live scripting tree
 into the RAW SAP MAP section of SAP_MAP. It does not lock CONFIG values.
 A user review is required before copying a candidate into the locked table.

TBD_BY_PREPLAN

TBD_BY_PREPLAN`
 Pure WT state engine.
 No SAP, Excel navigation, worksheet writes, Select, or Activate are allowed here.
 Remaining Qty is supplied by a later read-only reader; this module only decides
 the state from the values it receives.
WT_DONE
WT_ACTIVE
WT_CANCELLED_PENDING_REMAINING
WT_CANCELLED_REMAINING
WT_CANCELLED_ZERO

STOP_USER_ACTION
TBD_BY_PREPLAN
FALSE
TBD_BY_PREPLAN
 Normalize raw OB/WT/Remaining inputs into one deterministic state.
 PostingAllowed remains False in V1.2.1 because Posting is decided later by
 the pre-plan; the decision string exposes that it is still TBD for active rows.
WT_STATE_ERROR'
OB_DONE
OB_DONE has priority; WT is audit/display only.
OB_STATE_NOT_PROVEN
OB_OPEN_OR_PARTIAL
OB_STATE_NOT_PROVEN: OB header state is not proven.
WT status C: WT is already confirmed.
Blank WT status: active user-picking row.
REMAINING_QTY_REQUIRED: cancelled WT needs a proven Remaining Qty.'
REMAINING_QTY_NOT_PROVEN: Remaining Qty cannot be negative.
Cancelled WT has Remaining Qty > 0; reprocess is required.'
OB_QTY_ZERO_REQUIRES_USER_ACTION: cancelled WT has Remaining Qty=0.
UNKNOWN_WT_STATUS: raw WT status is not A, C, or blank.
 Compile/acceptance probe. It is pure logic only and intentionally writes nothing.

 Fail-safe: touching SAP GUI controls while the session is mid-repaint
 ("Busy") is the most common trigger of an untrappable "Automation error:
 Catastrophic failure". This version re-checks ses.Busy on every poll and
 only calls FindById once the session is confirmed idle.
SAP session is Nothing.

 Normal mode may reuse an already materialized WT grid.  In
 forced mode the caller explicitly requires one fresh action
 click, but the function must still succeed once that click
 has produced a valid grid.  The previous implementation
 skipped this return path whenever forceActionClick=True and
 therefore timed out on every forced navigation attempt.

 Central contract for SAP MAP_MODE / RUN_MODE separation.
 MAP_MODE is the only mode that may discover controls or use a mapper.
 RUN_MODE is fail-closed: it may use SAP GUI Scripting on exact mapped IDs,
 but it never falls back to captions, coordinates, SendKeys, or guessed IDs.
 Probe mode is read-only even when the caller is otherwise in RUN_MODE.
 Fail closed: a missing or unknown mode must never become RUN.
SAP_MODE
PROBE
LIVE_PILOTK
INVALID
 Exact-only safety mode used by shared SAP helpers.  PROBE, LIVE_PILOT,
 and INVALID are not RUN; they must be explicitly handled by their gate.
PROBE
SAP_PROBE_MODE
LIVE_PILOT
MAP_ALLOW_SCREEN_CONTROL
 Production default is hard-coded safe even if the CONFIG row is
 missing or malformed.
RUN_ALLOW_SCREEN_CONTROL

RUN_ALLOW_FALLBACK

 MAP probe is used only to validate a candidate map before it is locked.
 RUN probe is the later proof that the locked map has no screen/fallback
 dependency. Neither branch creates a session or performs a write.$

OURCE_LEGS
 V1.3B OPEN/READ-only evidence layer.
 Each entry point reuses an already-open SAP session, verifies only exact
 locked/candidate IDs, reads evidence, writes a local log, and stops.  No
 navigation, no new session/tab, no SendKeys, no Cancel/Save/Create/CF.

Confirm WT in Background
m WT in Background
 Single-pilot CF executor for the exact MON Warehouse Task flow.
 The executor is intentionally narrow: it selects one exact WT row, opens
 the row Methods menu, chooses the exact Background confirmation caption,
 then proves Status=C by a bounded read-back. No coordinates, SendKeys,
 caption search for navigation, posting, cancel, save, create, or retry.
Private Declare Sub CFEX_Sleep Lib "kernel32" Alias "Sleep" (ByVal dwMilliseconds As Long)

 Selecting the OB row and switching to the Warehouse Task tab both trigger
 a real SAP screen transition. A previous retry loop was accidentally
 configured as 1 To 1 even though its diagnostics claimed two attempts.
 Keep one bounded recovery retry, with no new SAP session/tab, so a transient
 control-tree rebuild cannot look like an Excel freeze.

 Bootstrap only: find an idle MON session or open /SCWM/MON automatically.

 Startup establishes only a usable idle MON session.  The currently
 visible result grid may be a stale layout from a previous activity, so
 it must never veto the next load. RT_LoadMON filters the requested OB
 and then verifies every exact technical column on that fresh result.

SAPGUI$
wnd[0]$
 First reuse an idle MON session. RUN mode never creates a SAP tab here:
 the executor may only reuse its verified PRDO tab through its dedicated
 same-tab recovery contract. This loader must fail closed rather than
 create a blank tab and attempt forbidden OK-code navigation.
 Never probe a session that is mid-repaint - this is
 the single most common trigger of a raw Catastrophic
 failure with SAP GUI Scripting.

PHASE1_WH
MON_RESULT_GRID_ID,MON_WT_GRID_ID,MON_UPPER_OB_COL,MON_UPPER_DOC_COL,MON_WT_DOC_COL,MON_WT_WHO_COL,MON_WT_COL,MON_WT_ITEM_COL,MON_WT_MATERIAL_COL,MON_WT_BATCH_COL,MON_WT_BIN_COL,MON_WT_QTY_COL,MON_WT_STATUS_COL,MON_OB_INPUT_ID,MON_EXECUTE_ID,MON_WT_ACTION_ID 
STOP_MON_SESSION_REQUIRED: open an idle /SCWM/MON session before loading release holders.
STOP_MON_SESSION_REQUIRED: existing MON session check failed. Err  
 The executor owns same-tab PRDO-to-MON recovery. This helper never creates
 a new SAP tab in RUN; a missing reader is a safe stop for other callers.
PHASE1_WH
MON_RESULT_GRID_ID,MON_WT_GRID_ID,MON_UPPER_OB_COL,MON_UPPER_DOC_COL,MON_WT_DOC_COL,MON_WT_WHO_COL,MON_WT_COL,MON_WT_ITEM_COL,MON_WT_MATERIAL_COL,MON_WT_BATCH_COL,MON_WT_BIN_COL,MON_WT_QTY_COL,MON_WT_STATUS_COL,MON_OB_INPUT_ID,MON_EXECUTE_ID,MON_WT_ACTION_ID 
STOP_MON_SESSION_REQUIRED: could not establish an idle /SCWM/MON reader session. 
STOP_MON_SESSION_REQUIRED: MON recovery failed. Err  
 The .children property itself (not just enumerating it) is a genuine COM
 call into the out-of-process SAP GUI Scripting engine. Reading it on a
 connection/session object that is stale, mid-teardown, or disconnected is
 one of the most common real-world triggers of an unwrapped, raw
 "-2147418113 Automation error: Catastrophic failure" - and it was NOT
 guarded before (only the enumeration helpers were), which is why the raw
 error kept escaping past every retry/try-catch layer around it.
 Single, safe access path (no more speculative multi-method fallback chain -
 repeatedly probing an out-of-process COM object with several different call
 styles back to back is what was poisoning the SAP GUI Scripting proxy and
 causing the untrappable "Catastrophic failure").

SelectedRows'
 The MON result row only needs to be selected before pressing the
 Warehouse Task toolbar button.  Clicking the hidden ERP_NUMBER_H cell
 causes a server transition/RPC error in this layout and is unnecessary.
SetCurrentCell'
MON_UPPER_DOC_COL
DOCNO_H
 ClickCurrentCell can trigger a full SAP screen transition (control tree
 torn down and rebuilt). Give SAP a moment to settle before touching the
 grid object again - this is the single most common source of a raw,
 untrappable Catastrophic failure in this exact spot.
 Scrolling the row into view is purely cosmetic - never abort the whole
 load just because this one call fails.

 User-facing CHECK planner.  V2.3 scheduler-contract build: CHECK writes thee
 execution/group metadata consumed by the locked outbound-WT scheduler.

 only through the existing read-only loader, then builds PLAN / source legs /
 release candidates. No Cancel, Save, Create, CF, MIGO, or SAP write exists.

 Group contract is deliberately outside the legacy A:AT lock metadata.  This

Scripting.Dictionary$
 CHECK is a deterministic local decision step.  It must never start,
 navigate, or reuse SAP /SCWM/POST implicitly.  A user-triggered
 TAI POSTING refreshes the authoritative cache; otherwise the plan
 remains stopped with the precise cache/capture reason.
 Resolve any batch inferred from the authoritative Posting snapshot before
 deriving group keys.  The executor must never receive a source group with
 a blank batch or a per-row quantity contract when the ODO item is shared.

A:AZ 
 The lock stores deterministic hashes of the immutable PLAN contract and the
 relevant WH_ACTUAL snapshot.  Button #4 recomputes both values before any
 SAP write, so a user edit after CHECK is a stale-plan stop rather than a
 reason for the executor to re-plan.
 Lock only the MON source snapshot and user-owned picking inputs.
 O:S and Y:AD are derived status/result fields written by CHECK and
 the executor.  Including them made a completed prior OB invalidate
 the same CHECK lock before the next OB could run.

wnd[0]/usr/subSUB_COMPLETE_OIP:/SCWM/SAPLUI_DLV_PRD:2000/subSUB_OIP_DATA_AREA:/SCWM/SAPLUI_DLV_PRD:2210/cntlCONTAINER_TB_OIP_1/shellcont/shell
OIP_CHANGE
wnd[0]/usr/subSUB_COMPLETE_ODP1:/SCWM/SAPLUI_DLV_PRD:3000/tabsTABSTRIP_ODP1/tabpOK_ODP1_TAB1/ssubSUB_ODP1_TAB1:/SCWM/SAPLUI_DLV_CORE:3210/ssubSUB_ODP1_1_CONTENT:/SCWM/SAPLUI_DLV_CORE:3211/cntlCONTAINER_ALV_ODP1_1/shellcont/shell
 Generic rework runner.  It derives one exact context from the sole PASSa
 row for the OB currently entered on INPUT.  It never confirms a WT and
 always stops at CF_USER_HANDOFF.
 PRDO target and WT-create quantity are deliberately separate for DELTA_ADD.
 Example: an existing open WT 60 is retained, PRDO is raised to 280, and the
 new WT is created only for the incremental 220.
 A nonblank value is used only by the OB queue to bind every re-entrant
 helper call to one exact PLAN row.  The regular single-case entry points
 deliberately leave it blank and retain their one-ready-plan contract.
 Set only while the controlled multi-WT executor is cancelling a complete
 batch.  A peer old WT is expected to remain active until its own turn; it
 must not be confused with an unrelated new outbound WT.
 These counters describe bounded MON navigation for the current ex
ecutor run.
 They never cause a SAP write and are reset before each top-level route.
 Per-OB idempotency counters.  They are reset at the scheduler boundary and
 are deliberately kept in memory only; RUN_HISTORY remains the durable proof.
 A second PRDO Save for the same document/item in one cycle is blocked before
 the SAP write rather than silently becoming a duplicate action.
 True only while a prior POST Create+Save is being reconciled.  This is a
 read-only recovery context: it may tolerate a missing target-batch PLAN field
 long enough to query SAP, but it never authorizes a second POST write.
 Scheduler execution-context cache.  One locked OB run may revisit the same
 plan row from several phase gates; reloading PLAN/WH_ACTUAL on every visit
 used to reset the live context and made the SAP tab appear to reopen.  The
 cache is scoped to one run and one immutable plan fingerprint only.

V2.4-GROUP-EXECUTOR-WHTASK-REUSE
 Source addendum for the in-workbook modCodexWTRework module.
 Opens the standard Distribute Quantity surface only on a temporary manual
 draft, maps it, then cancels that surface and deletes the temporary draft.
 It never confirms a distribution, saves, creates a WT, or calls CF.

 Local-only reference for every inferred source-bin branch.  This is a
 decision contract, not an executor: it neither attaches to SAP nor writes
 to a worksheet.  A new OB must still pass its own exact snapshot/preflight.
BRANCH=DIRECT_CREATE_WT|WHEN=Exactly one eligible source row at the requested bin already has the WH/ODO batch and the full requested quantity|NEXT=Create one in-memory draft; prove reservation; Save once; read MON; stop for user CF 
BRANCH=POST_BATCH_CHANGE_REQUIRED|WHEN=No eligible WH/ODO batch exists at the requested bin, but exactly one eligible alternate batch row exists there|NEXT=Create only the guarded posting-change WT; stop for user CF; after user CF, re-read and classify again
BRANCH=STOP_INSUFFICIENT_CORRECT_BATCH|WHEN=The WH/ODO batch exists at the requested bin but its available or total quantity is below the request|NEXT=Do not split, mix batches, or save; user resolves quantity/stock first
BRANCH=STOP_AMBIGUOUS_CORRECT_BATCH|WHEN=More than one eligible row has the correct WH/ODO batch|NEXT=Do not auto-select; user identifies the intended stock row
BRANCH=STOP_AMBIGUOUS_ALTERNATE_BATCH|WHEN=More than one eligible alternate-batch row exists at the requested bin|NEXT=Do not auto-post batch change; user identifies the intended alternate batch row
BRANCH=STOP_NO_ELIGIBLE_STOCK_AT_TARGET_BIN|WHEN=No eligible source row exists at the requested bin|NEXT=Do not create WT or posting change; user resolves stock/bin first
BRANCH=ACTIVE_OUTBOUND_WT_EXISTS|WHEN=MON already shows an active outbound WT for the exact ODO item|NEXT=No create or retry; read back only and hand CF to the user
BRANCH=STALE_TOOL_OWNED_DRAFT_ONLY|WHEN=Create screen shows an unsaved draft but MON proves no active outbound WT|NEXT=Discard only the tool-owned draft without Save, then re-run the read-only plan
RULE=CF|OWNER=USER|AUTOMATION=NEVER_CONFIRM_WT
PASS|REWORK_FLOW_MATRIX|SAP=NOT_ACCESSED|WRITE=FALSE|CF=USER_HANDOFF

PICK_BIN_POST_START_MODAL_PRESENT: 
 Never refresh a visible POST session over somebody else's pending draft.
 A POST Create+Save checkpoint is a strict one-shot boundary.  Before
 considering another plan state, re-run the exact product query and use
 only the newly returned stock to reconcile it.  The visible POST grid
 can be cleared or re-bound by SAP after Create+Save; reusing it caused a
 false "SOURCE_MATCH_COUNT=0 / TARGET_EXISTS=0" stop even after the
 business result had settled.  This branch has no write action.

 at the exact pick bin with enough quantity.  Never issue POST again.

POST_SESSION_NOT_SET'
 CHECK + POSTING has already loaded the exact product grid.  Cancelling a
 WT does not alter stock at the target bin, so reuse that live, visible
 grid after validating the material instead of re-pressing a legacy
 search-button control.  A forced recovery refresh must still obtain a
 new result, but it must not rewrite the Product field when that grid is
 already correct: on this SAP GUI patch that setter can raise error 613.
REUSE_CURRENT_PRODUCT_GRID'
 F8 is the stable read-only Execute/query action for the already
 validated product context.  Do not touch the repainting MATNR
 input field here; this is the exact recovery path that formerly
 stopped at STEP=SET_PRODUCT with SAP GUI error 613.

SET_PRODUCT

 stock result.  Re-open the same read-only POST transaction once,
 reacquire the exact controls, then retry SET_PRODUCT exactly one
 time.  No mutation is sent from this recovery.

MON_UPPER_GRID_NOT_FOUND:  
 A filtered MON layout can legitimately retain other visible document

MON_FILTER 
FAIL 
 MON returns identifier cells as strings; normalize leading zero padding only
 so the exact document remains the identity gate in every layout.
 Sends one Cancel WT action for the complete selected-row set.  The

BATCH_SOURCE_FIND_ERROR: 
 The source-stock grids expose both CAT and TO_CAT on some SAP layouts.  The
 POST precheck uses TO_CAT as the source-of-truth, so Create WT must use the
 same precedence or it can reject the exact row that precheck already proved.
TO_CAT$
 UI_VSOLA is an alternative-UoM source quantity.  Prefer the matching
 available-quantity fields, then use only established fallbacks from the
 bounded read-only grid contract.  Every text value must pass the canonical
 SAP parser so callers can reject malformed availability explicitly.

PLAN_LOCK_READ_ERROR:  
 Keep the lock tied to immutable MON data plus user-owned picking
 inputs. O:S and Y:AD are derived status/result fields that CHECK or
 the executor updates after an earlier OB; hashing them falsely
 blocked the next OB in the same WH snapshot.
 Compatibility hash for a PLAN locked before the status-field exclusion was
 introduced. It is used only to recognize and safely migrate an old lock;
 new CHECK runs always write CWR_WHActualContractHash instead.
 When the source/user fields still match the locked PLAN, an old full-row
 hash may be migrated after the executor has changed only derived status
 fields. This does not re-plan and never permits a changed source or input.
 Compatibility bridge for a PLAN locked by the immediately previous release.
 That release deliberately wrote BLOCKED_DELTA_EXECUTOR_REQUIRED because the
 executor did not exist yet.  Promote only that exact, otherwise-PASS contract
 to READY; this is not replanning and the delta route still performs fresh
 Monitor, POST-source, and PRDO readbacks before its first SAP write.
CHANGE_QTY

PLAN_EXECUTABLE|LEGACY_DELTA_PROMOTED= 
 Processes all safe, independent Plan rows for the OB currently entered on
 INPUT.  Status C/WT_DONE is recorded as completed and never touched.  Status
 A is eligible only as the known cancelled-resume state; its remaining
 quantity is freshly read by the exact case route before any later write.
 A local blocker is reported and blocks only its ODO-item group; an uncertain
 SAP outcome always ends the queue without retry.
 Retained only as historical source evidence.  Button #4 exits above into
 the locked-plan phase scheduler and can no longer run this per-Plan
 full-flow loop or its special batch branch.

 Button #4 is a locked-plan scheduler.  It performs no classification,
 source selection, release choice, QtyMode derivation or re-plan.  Those
 choices are contractual outputs of CHECK.  The SAP routines called below are
 the existing bounded executors; this layer only controls their phase/order.

 A Create+Save action is never shared by different materials.  Older
 PLAN rows carried a short CreateGroup such as
 CREATE|OB|DOC|ITEM, which let one blocked material suppress every
 other material in the same document.  Keep the checked group label
 for auditability, but make the scheduler key immutable at the
 business-action scope (OB + document + item + material).

 This member owns a prior POST write boundary, not a new
 route.  It participates in the shared quantity aggregate but
 has no cancel/qty action until the location recovery proves
 the final SAP batch.

GROUP_PREFLIGHT
 A shared QTY group can legitimately contain a cancel/replace member
 alongside a retained delta member.  The old scheduler rejected that
 composition as GROUP_EXEC_MODE_MIXED before it ever reached Cancel.
 The safe contract is phase-based instead: verify retained members,
 cancel every replace/zero old WT first, then let the later location
 phase perform POST/change-bin and the later qty phase perform PRDO.

 Build one fresh, exact cancellation queue for only the members
 that own an old WT.  No SAP write occurs until every such member
 is known active or already cancelled.  This filtered queue is
 what makes mixed cancel+location+qty groups safe and prevents
 a preflight mode mismatch from skipping required cancellations.

 True PRDO DocumentGroup executor.  CHECK may put several quantity/source
 contracts under one document/item package; all readbacks are completed first
 and the SAP PRDO surface is then saved at most once for that document group.
 The older QTY-group routine below remains as a compatibility route for the
 single-case executor, but Button #4 uses this document-level scheduler.
 Do not gate the whole document on one member.  Each QTY group below
 owns its own readback/write contract; a blocked member is preserved
 in place and independent groups continue through this document.

 A toolbar Press can be queued before Session.Busy flips.  Do not issue a
 follow-on query immediately: wait through a short stable window and then
 require idle, with an explicit ceiling to avoid a hung SAP session.
 This audit is intentionally workbook-local.  It does not obtain a SAP
 session, navigate SAP, or send an action.  LIVE_PROVEN can only be supplied
 by an earlier recorded live regression, never inferred from a compile.

 A new scenario may remain NOT_PROVEN, but a scenario that has previously
 been independently marked LIVE_PROVEN must not degrade to FAIL or
 NOT_PROVEN.  This is called before any executor reaches a SAP write.

 unrelated scenario is intentionally NOT_APPLICABLE and must not block a
 different business route (for example prior increase proof vs decrease).

 MIXED_OB is diagnostic only.  It must never be a whole-OB gate:
 each active member is already represented by its own required
 business scenario (CREATE/CANCEL/CHANGE_QTY).  Durable DONE,
 SKIP and unrelated blocked rows therefore cannot stop the active
 work merely because the document contains more than one mode.

CF_ONLY
 A durable PLAN group can contain rows already completed by the user
 or blocked for a separate release.  When the current execution owns
 only a subset, audit that subset's action contract; otherwise the
 old rows contaminate Sum(Sys)/Sum(Target) and create false failures.

CF_ONLY
Qty/Bin/Batch unchanged; no SAP write
CANCEL_TO_ZERO_SINGLE
One CANCEL_PRDO_ZERO has TargetQty=0 
MULTI_CANCEL
Every queued cancellation has TargetQty=0
CHANGE_QTY_DECREASE
Plan group delta is negative and aggregate-consistent
CHANGE_QTY_INCREASE
Plan group delta is positive and aggregate-consistent
CREATE_WT_DELTA
CreateQty equals TargetQty minus EffectiveAfterRelease 
MIXED_OB
Mixed action group has a consistent projected state
FINAL_GROUP_VERIFY
Sum(Sys)=GroupCurrent and Sum(Target)=GroupTarget
 Regression must describe the work still owned by this scheduler execution.
 PLAN is intentionally durable and retains rows already completed by the user
 or blocked for a separate release/CF handoff.  Counting those historical
 rows as current action modes creates false MIXED_OB/FINAL_GROUP failures and
 can stop the run before any SAP write.  Only active executor modes in an
 actionable state participate in the gate.
